###Seasons Scripted Effects###


## Season Changes Scripted Effects##

#Winter#

set_AGOT_season_winter = {
	every_province = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				geographical_region = world_group_one_b
				geographical_region = world_group_two 						
				geographical_region = world_group_three
				geographical_region = world_group_four
				geographical_region = world_group_five
				geographical_region = world_group_six
				geographical_region = world_group_seven
				geographical_region = world_group_eight
				geographical_region = world_group_nine
				geographical_region = world_group_ten
				geographical_region = world_group_one
			}
		}
		remove_province_modifier = cold_autumn_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = wet_autumn_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = autumn_modifier
		#remove_province_modifier = cool_autumn_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = dry_autumn_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = dry_spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = hot_spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = cool_spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = wet_spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = cold_spring_modifier

		remove_province_modifier = cold_autumn_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = wet_autumn_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = autumn_modifier_s
		#remove_province_modifier = cool_autumn_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = dry_autumn_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = dry_spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = hot_spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = cool_spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = wet_spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = cold_spring_modifier_s

	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_game_rule = light_income_penalty
		}

#Spring#
set_AGOT_season_spring = {
	every_province = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				geographical_region = world_group_one_b
				geographical_region = world_group_two 						
				geographical_region = world_group_three
				geographical_region = world_group_four
				geographical_region = world_group_five
				geographical_region = world_group_six
				geographical_region = world_group_seven
				geographical_region = world_group_eight
				geographical_region = world_group_nine
				geographical_region = world_group_ten
				geographical_region = world_group_one
			}
		}
		remove_province_modifier = winter_normal_modifier_1
		remove_province_modifier = winter_cold_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = winter_light_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = winter_southern_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = winter_north_modifier
	}
	situation:agot_seasons = {
		situation_sub_region:westeros_barrowlands = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					change_phase = seasons_spring_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_spring = yes
					}
				}
				25 = {
					change_phase = seasons_cool_spring_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_cool_spring = yes
					}
				}
				5 = {
					change_phase = seasons_cold_spring_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_cold_spring = yes
					}
				}
				15 = {
					change_phase = seasons_wet_spring_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_wet_spring = yes
					}
				}
				10 = {
					change_phase = seasons_dry_spring_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_dry_spring = yes
					}
				}
				1 = {
					change_phase = seasons_hot_spring_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_hot_spring = yes
					}
				}				
			}
		}

#Summer#
set_AGOT_season_summer = {
	every_province = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				geographical_region = world_group_one_b
				geographical_region = world_group_two 						
				geographical_region = world_group_three
				geographical_region = world_group_four
				geographical_region = world_group_five
				geographical_region = world_group_six
				geographical_region = world_group_seven
				geographical_region = world_group_eight
				geographical_region = world_group_nine
				geographical_region = world_group_ten
				geographical_region = world_group_one
			}
		}
		remove_province_modifier = cold_spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = wet_spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = hot_spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = cool_spring_modifier
		remove_province_modifier = dry_spring_modifier		
		
		remove_province_modifier = cold_spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = wet_spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = hot_spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = cool_spring_modifier_s
		remove_province_modifier = dry_spring_modifier_s
	}
	situation:agot_seasons = {
		situation_sub_region:westeros_barrowlands = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					change_phase = seasons_summer_phase

						limit = { 
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						} 
						make_it_summer = yes
					}
				}

					change_phase = seasons_wet_summer_phase

					}

				}			
			}
		}

#and Fall (Autumn)
set_AGOT_season_autumn_start = {
	situation:agot_seasons = {
		situation_sub_region:westeros_beyond_the_wall_region = {
				change_phase = seasons_always_winter_phase
		}
		situation_sub_region:westeros_barrowlands = {
			random_list = {
				25 = {
					change_phase = seasons_autumn_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_autumn = yes
					}
				}
				10 = {
					change_phase = seasons_wet_autumn_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_wet_autumn = yes
					}
				}
				15 = {
					change_phase = seasons_dry_autumn_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_dry_autumn = yes
					}
				}
				40 = {
					change_phase = seasons_cold_autumn_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_cold_autumn = yes
					}
				}					
			}
		}

set_AGOT_Season_Winter_Startup = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_game_rule = light_income_penalty
		}
		situation:agot_seasons = {
			situation_sub_region:westeros_beyond_the_wall_region = {
				change_phase = seasons_always_winter_phase
			}
			every_province = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						geographical_region = world_group_one
						geographical_region = world_group_two
						geographical_region = world_group_three						
						geographical_region = world_group_four
						geographical_region = world_group_five
						geographical_region = world_group_six
					}
					
				}
				add_province_modifier = winter_light_modifier
			}
			situation_sub_region:westeros_the_wall = {
				change_phase = seasons_light_winter_phase
			}
			situation_sub_region:westeros_barrowlands = {
				change_phase = seasons_light_winter_phase
			}			
			situation_sub_region:westeros_stoney_shore = {
				change_phase = seasons_light_winter_phase
			}			
			situation_sub_region:westeros_wolfswood = {
				change_phase = seasons_light_winter_phase
			}

			}

			}

set_AGOT_season_spring_start = {
	situation:agot_seasons = {
			situation_sub_region:westeros_beyond_the_wall_region = {
				change_phase = seasons_always_winter_phase
			}
		situation_sub_region:westeros_barrowlands = {
			random_list = {
				10 = {
					change_phase = seasons_spring_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_spring = yes
					}
				}
				25 = {
					change_phase = seasons_cool_spring_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {
							geographical_region = world_barrowlands_seasons
						}
						make_it_cool_spring = yes
					}
				}
				5 = {
					change_phase = seasons_cold_spring_phase
					every_province = {
						limit = {

####WORK IN PROGRESS
#### NEW SNOW CONTINGENCY MECHANIC

set_AGOT_season_winter_NEW = {

##set thenn valley to always winter variant
##set frost fangs to always winter variant
##set haunted forest to always winter variant
##set frozen shore to always winter variant

##Snow Group One : 

##Set Regions 5,6,7,8,9,10,11,12,13 to winter with snow
## 95% chance of Upper Vale having snow
## if upper vale has snow, then 90% chance for western Highland
## All region 1 has snow go to effect 2
	set_AGOT_season_winter_group_1 = yes
	set_AGOT_season_winter_group_2 = yes
	#Partial group 3
	every_province = {
		limit = {
			geographical_region = world_group_one
		}
		add_province_modifier = winter_north_modifier
	}
	every_province = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				#geographical_region = world_group_one_b
				#geographical_region = world_group_two 						
				geographical_region = world_group_three
				geographical_region = world_group_four
				geographical_region = world_group_five
				geographical_region = world_group_six
				geographical_region = world_group_seven
				geographical_region = world_group_eight
				geographical_region = world_group_nine
				geographical_region = world_group_ten
			}
